<!-- ZS-POST {"url":"https://community.zscaler.com/s/question/0D54u00009evlBSCAY/thoughts-on-top-challenges-with-traditional-data-protection","lastmod":"2023-07-06T12:54:20.000Z","id":"0D54u00009evlBSCAY"} -->
## Thoughts on top challenges with traditional data protection

- Source: https://community.zscaler.com/s/question/0D54u00009evlBSCAY/thoughts-on-top-challenges-with-traditional-data-protection
- Type: Q&A
- Posted: 2022-10-13T15:52:29.000Z
- Last activity: 2023-07-06T12:54:20.000Z
- Answers: 2
- Likes: 1
- Note: ユーザー投稿であり Zscaler の公式見解ではない。内容が古い場合があるため投稿日を確認すること。
- Note: 回答本文は UI API では取得できないため未収録。

With the proliferation of cloud apps, data now lives in your various applications, and employees access this data from several remote locations. This makes traditional data protection controls inadequate in controlling your data.

Here is just a small list of challenges on how traditional data protection controls are unable to help you control your data.

Unable to properly follow your users due to your cloud apps being accessed from any number of locations. Which means, no longer within your network or controls.

Visibility into compliance within cloud apps is difficult because your cloud apps are spread across multiple locations. This means you have no state awareness of compliance.

Traditional data protection approaches are unable to inspect SSL/TLS traffic. With most traffic being encrypted, not being able to inspect SSL/TLS traffic leaves you blind to potential risks.

Point solutions turn into “spaghetti stacks? with time. This creates unnecessary complexity and doesn’t provide a unified view needed to understand exposure.

These challenges alone are already reasons to look for better solutions. If you then add in the complexity and knowledge gaps in order to solve these problems, you end up with a project that appears to always have a constrain on resources.

What other challenges have you experienced with traditional data protection controls? Interested in learning more about what experienced professionals in the day-to-day can share about these hurdles.
<!-- /ZS-POST -->

Does anyone know how to create a dynamic location group which could contain both location and sublocations (from that location) matching certain criterias?

Example:
 We have the following locations/sublocations
 ACME-a-city1
 ACME-a-city1->foo
 ACME-a-city1->other
 ACME-a-city2
 ACME-b-city1
 ACME-b-city1->bar
 ACME-b-city1->foo
 ACME-b-city1->other
 ACME-b-city2
 ACME-b-city2->smth
 ACME-b-city2->other

I want to create a dynamic location group which contains all listed above with name starting with ‘ACME-b-’, both locations and sublocations - but NOT anything starting with ‘ACME-A’

Means the dyn group should contain only
 ACME-b-city1
 ACME-b-city1->bar
 ACME-b-city1->baz
 ACME-b-city1->other
 ACME-b-city2
 ACME-b-city2->smth
 ACME-b-city2->other

Setting the criteria for ‘name starts with’ does not work; sublocations are missed then.
 Setting the criteria for ‘name contains/equals/… other’ does not work either; then also ‘ACME-a-city1-> other’ is matched

Clearly that does not exist…

Example - In the past my Work IP may be 192.168.x.x.

<!-- ZS-POST {"url":"https://community.zscaler.com/s/question/0D54u00009evmefCAA/strange-network-mapped-drive-access-issue","lastmod":"2023-05-31T08:43:27.000Z","id":"0D54u00009evmefCAA"} -->
## Strange network mapped drive access issue

- Source: https://community.zscaler.com/s/question/0D54u00009evmefCAA/strange-network-mapped-drive-access-issue
- Type: Q&A
- Posted: 2023-01-29T18:33:36.000Z
- Last activity: 2023-05-31T08:43:27.000Z
- Answers: 1
- Likes: 0
- Note: ユーザー投稿であり Zscaler の公式見解ではない。内容が古い場合があるため投稿日を確認すること。
- Note: 回答本文は UI API では取得できないため未収録。

This issue happened to us twice,

Here is the situation: Internal on-premise AD server and azure cloud virtual AD server. On-premise file storage and zsure file storage.

Case one: we have AD logon scripts mapping the local on premise storage as network drive. When we changed the logon scripts for network mapping drives (DNS name only, same file server). Can not access the mapped drive anymore. We found out the solution is uninstalling the zscaler network adapter.

Case two: Last week, we switched the mapped drive from internal file server to azure filestorage. Everthing seems working, only few users need to reinstall the zscaler. So I forced users to upgrade the zscaler from 3.9.x to 4.1.xx, then most of the users have issues accessing the mapped drive. I need to uninstall the zscaler and install the zscaler again to fix the issue. Few hundred users, really painful.
 For the users in the office (By pass zscaler), network reset will take care the issue, but not for remote users. The whole computer is slow and no mapped drive can be accessed until uninstall and reinstall zscaler again.

Can not just reinstall zscaler, must uninstall first.
 Other internet access like [box.net](http://box.net), no issues at all.
 We did test some users, no issues (We didn’t upgrade the zscaler 3.9 to 4.1)
 I have AAD devices (No AD joined), no issues.
 I have 30 windows CPC computers (No zscaler installed), no issues

Any clues? Thanks.
<!-- /ZS-POST -->

I connect to the 5g wifi on wifi but regular get zscaller disconnecting. My system try show me connect to a local network and also shows my usual wifi connection. <!-- ZS-POST {"url":"https://community.zscaler.com/s/question/0D54u00009evmfjCAA/forum-category-changes-in-january","lastmod":"2023-07-07T11:22:21.000Z","id":"0D54u00009evmfjCAA"} -->
## Forum Category Changes in January

- Source: https://community.zscaler.com/s/question/0D54u00009evmfjCAA/forum-category-changes-in-january
- Type: Q&A
- Posted: 2022-12-20T16:19:26.000Z
- Last activity: 2023-07-07T11:22:21.000Z
- Answers: 5
- Likes: 5
- Note: ユーザー投稿であり Zscaler の公式見解ではない。内容が古い場合があるため投稿日を確認すること。
- Note: 回答本文は UI API では取得できないため未収録。

We are moving into the second phase of the Information Architecture restructuring for the Zenith Community. You have already seen some of the changes that are part of this restructuring last week. If you missed those updates, check out the [new cool changes to the forum navigation](https://community.zscaler.com/t/some-cool-changes-to-zenith-community-navigation/19867).

Starting the **first week of January** you are going to see new categories available that are aligned better with our product offerings. This effort is to help you find information quicker and post topics in areas that are more easily identifiable.

When the new categories go live, we will also restrict the old categories to **Replies Only**. This will allow for conversations that live in legacy categories to continue as is. New topics will be created in the new Product categories.

After the categories have been created, we will start the process of moving topics from the old categories into the new categories and ensuring proper tags are applied.

**How you can help**
 As you continue to use the forum and the new category structure please always provide feedback on the availability of categories. This will help us identify needed areas of the community and start to formulate a better knowledge asset.

Additionally, if you want to move your own topics, you can do so easily by editing the title of the topic and changing the category instead of the topic title.

**What’s this new structure?**
 The new structure is aligned to product features and capabilities. This will help with quickly finding the area to find your answer or ask your questions.

[[image] image1056×1240 112 KB](https://community.zscaler.com/zenith/servlet/servlet.FileDownload?file=0154u000009O8IsAAK)

**Tags**
 Tags in the new categories will be restricted to topic type. Tags are still optional for the time being. These tags will help us categorize types of topic posts for future categorization.
 [image]

If you have any questions or concerns please let us know! We want to build a community that helps you all become the best Zero Trust and Zscaler experts!
<!-- /ZS-POST -->

---

## Access an internal app that uses internal network using aws lambda function and zscaler

- Source: https://community.zscaler.com/s/question/0D54u00009evmh9CAA/access-an-internal-app-that-uses-internal-network-using-aws-lambda-function-and-zscaler
- Type: Q&A
- Posted: 2022-11-19T20:16:28.000Z
- Last activity: 2023-05-31T08:12:53.000Z
- Answers: 0
- Likes: 0
- Note: ユーザー投稿であり Zscaler の公式見解ではない。内容が古い場合があるため投稿日を確認すること。
- Note: 回答本文は UI API では取得できないため未収録。

Hello, I would really appreciate any feedback from the community.

Let us assume the following:

1. I have an internal web app running on an internal private network
2. I can connect to that web app via two ways:
  - from the internal network without zscaler being on: before redirecting to the web app, zscaler pops up where the credentials are provided automatically in the HTTP GET request header, then authenticates, then get me the HTTP response in a JSON.
  - from any network (except for portals) with zcaler on (private access on), and then the same thing (mentioned in the point just before this) is repeated .

The question is: Let us say I have a Python code deployed via AWS Lambda. Is there a way to make the HTTP request using zscaler?

Thank you.
<!-- /ZS-POST -->

<!-- ZS-POST {"url":"https://community.zscaler.com/s/question/0D54u00009evmhECAQ/so-thankful-for-zenith-community-and-all-our-members","lastmod":"2023-07-06T11:43:09.000Z","id":"0D54u00009evmhECAQ"} -->
## So thankful for Zenith Community and all our members!

- Source: https://community.zscaler.com/s/question/0D54u00009evmhECAQ/so-thankful-for-zenith-community-and-all-our-members
- Type: Q&A
- Posted: 2022-11-17T18:12:27.000Z
- Last activity: 2023-07-06T11:43:09.000Z
- Answers: 1
- Likes: 4
- Note: ユーザー投稿であり Zscaler の公式見解ではない。内容が古い場合があるため投稿日を確認すること。
- Note: 回答本文は UI API では取得できないため未収録。

As the cost of living soars each day, families in need are having to make the difficult decision between paying bills and feeding their families. This should never be a choice. Every family deserves to eat healthy food.

Yesterday, [@Cjablonski](https://community.zscaler.com/u/cjablonski) and I volunteered our afternoon to help out families in need at [Silicon Valley Second Harvest](https://www.shfb.org/). We were representing our individual teams and our amazing company, Zscaler.

In 3 hours, 29 of us had sorted 31,500 lbs of fresh produce and packaged them in neat boxes. To offer some perspective on why this number matters, 31,500 lbs of food provides for over 5000 people in need for a whole week. Individually, each one of us sorted and handled 1100 lbs of produce! Second Harvest is now providing nutritious food to over 400,000 people each month, a 60% increase over pre-pandemic numbers.

In the end, it was a true feeling of accomplishment, of happiness, and selflessness. We all deserve to feel this way! Thank you Team Agilent, for including us in your group!

Giving back should remain a huge part of who we are as a society and an important tradition that we hand down to the next generation.

As we in America head towards our Thanksgiving next week, **I want to thank each and every community member for being a part of our amazing community**, for your valuable time that you give to help others in our community, and for the thankfulness I feel leading this incredible journey along with our Knowledge Manager, [@Ben_Garrison](https://community.zscaler.com/u/ben_garrison).

**Please share your volunteering stories in the comments below**. Yes, pictures are very welcome. Let us unite in showing the world that we care!

#givingback #zscalerlife #zenithcommunity #zscaler

<!-- ZS-POST {"url":"https://community.zscaler.com/s/question/0D54u00009evmhGCAQ/connection-error-using-sim-card","lastmod":"2026-06-27T11:47:03.000Z","id":"0D54u00009evmhGCAQ"} -->
## Connection error using SIM Card

- Source: https://community.zscaler.com/s/question/0D54u00009evmhGCAQ/connection-error-using-sim-card
- Type: Q&A
- Posted: 2022-11-17T07:26:24.000Z
- Last activity: 2026-06-27T11:47:03.000Z
- Answers: 3
- Likes: 1
- Note: ユーザー投稿であり Zscaler の公式見解ではない。内容が古い場合があるため投稿日を確認すること。
- Note: 回答本文は UI API では取得できないため未収録。

Hi all,
 since a few days we have some issues around the world when our users need to use their SIM Cards.
 ZCC is not able to establish a connection towards the ZCloud.
 This issue is with different SIM Card providers from Germany, Italy etc.
 We figured out, if you use the SIM Card in another Notebook, the issue is following the SIM Card.

In Germany we also discovered if some users are customer of Deutsche Telekom and using the new Router from Telekom there is a feature called LTE Bonding, something like “lite SDWAN?. The LTE and WAN connection are used simultaneously. If you disable this feature, everything is working fine.

We already have multipe open Tickets with Zscaler but no resulution.

Is anyone else facing similar issues when using the SIM Card connection in a Notebook and maybe have a fix/solution/workaround?

Cheers Jonas
<!-- /ZS-POST -->

---

We use a third-party SaaS application (not included in the list [here](https://help.zscaler.com/zia/adding-saas-application-tenants)) that we want to only allow users to access through Zscaler. For example, if Zscaler includes a signature in user requests routed through Zscaler and forwarded to the SaaS application, the SaaS application could verify the signature before providing access to our data. As far as I can tell though, that’s not an option.

Thank you for your help!

*With* [*Data Protection Transformed*](https://www.zscaler.com/data-protection-transformed)*, Zscaler has released industry-first innovations that transform how you classify and protect data. Watch our launch broadcast to get a front-row seat to this action-packed event.*

## How to add an exception to Zscaler IP rerouting for logging in to Salesforce?

- Source: https://community.zscaler.com/s/question/0D54u00009evmi0CAA/how-to-add-an-exception-to-zscaler-ip-rerouting-for-logging-in-to-salesforce
- Type: Q&A
- Posted: 2022-10-14T16:06:34.000Z
- Last activity: 2023-05-31T08:44:22.000Z
- Answers: 1
- Likes: 0
- Note: ユーザー投稿であり Zscaler の公式見解ではない。内容が古い場合があるため投稿日を確認すること。
- Note: 回答本文は UI API では取得できないため未収録。

Hi everyone,

I’m asking here because my Zscaler team seems stumped and I am not able to log into a partner’s salesforce. Our network is using the Zsclaer client connector, and Zscaler will re-route traffic to show as coming from the Zscaler Server instead of our actual server location.

I need to log in to [login.salesforce.com](http://login.salesforce.com) which is being operated by one of our partner organizations, but they are only willing to whitelist our actual server IP, not the Zscaler Server IP. Hence, we have set up exceptions to Zscaler for the URLS they provided, and for Salesforce in particular we tried to put in exceptions to Zscaler to not reroute traffic from addresses like Salesforce dot com , c dot salesforce dot com, or their location cookie, ([https://geolocation.onetrust.com/cookieconsentpub/v1/geo/location](https://geolocation.onetrust.com/cookieconsentpub/v1/geo/location)), but Salesforce still shows the login attempt as coming from the Zscaler server IP.

Does anyone here have experience with configuring Zscaler to NOT take over traffic going to salesforce? Also, my apologies if this isn’t the correct forum to ask a question like this.

Thank you for any help on the topic.
<!-- /ZS-POST -->

There is an update to our community platform that we will upgrade to tonight at 7PM EST. This is a pretty straightforward update that is done with just a few clicks, so the infamous last words, “this shouldn’t take very long? (said no IT admin ever).

<!-- ZS-POST {"url":"https://community.zscaler.com/s/question/0D54u00009evmkFCAQ/community-feedback-necroposting","lastmod":"2023-07-06T12:56:46.000Z","id":"0D54u00009evmkFCAQ"} -->
## Community Feedback: Necroposting

- Source: https://community.zscaler.com/s/question/0D54u00009evmkFCAQ/community-feedback-necroposting
- Type: Q&A
- Posted: 2022-07-26T12:57:18.000Z
- Last activity: 2023-07-06T12:56:46.000Z
- Answers: 5
- Likes: 0
- Note: ユーザー投稿であり Zscaler の公式見解ではない。内容が古い場合があるため投稿日を確認すること。
- Note: 回答本文は UI API では取得できないため未収録。

One of the things that plagues a few online communities is what is called “necroposting?. If you’re not familiar with this term, it’s basically when a topic is “brought back to life? after being dead for a long period of time.

On the surface, this doesn’t seem like a huge problem. But it can quickly make a community like ours hard to follow and find things that are current and related. Imagine seeing the “latest? feed filled with topics that are over two years old, and the only reason they are there is because someone replied with “Yeah, I’m also dealing with this?.

So my question to the community is pretty straight forward. What do you all consider a “dead post? to be? Is it a length of time? Time after it is has been resolved etc?

I can think of many moderation policies but ultimately I want to make sure that our existing users and community members are able to provide feedback before we make a change like this.

So please, send me your input so we can start to find a solid solution.
<!-- /ZS-POST -->

---

## [VIDEO] Community Author Spotlight - Zero Trust Architecture

- Source: https://community.zscaler.com/s/question/0D54u00009evmklCAA/video-community-author-spotlight-zero-trust-architecture
- Type: Q&A
- Posted: 2022-07-12T21:25:50.000Z
- Last activity: 2023-05-31T09:08:58.000Z
- Answers: 1
- Likes: 5
- Note: ユーザー投稿であり Zscaler の公式見解ではない。内容が古い場合があるため投稿日を確認すること。
- Note: 回答本文は UI API では取得できないため未収録。

Authors [@Nathan_Howe](https://community.zscaler.com/u/nathan_howe), [@sganguli](https://community.zscaler.com/u/sganguli) and [@gfesta](https://community.zscaler.com/u/gfesta) step into our Community Spotlight to discuss the book “The Seven Elements of Highly Successful Zero Trust Architecture?.

You can find more information on the book on our **[Zscaler site](https://www.zscaler.com/seven-elements-of-zero-trust)** . To request an ebook download, please click on this **[download link](https://info.zscaler.com/resources-ebook-seven-elements-of-highly-successful-zta?_gl=1*1p5k4h4*_ga*MzI5NjY3NjE2LjE2NTc2NjMzMzE.*_ga_10SPJ4YJL9*MTY3Mzk5MTk5OC40NjUuMS4xNjczOTkyMTQ5LjU1LjAuMA..&_ga=2.209057435.847554905.1673894904-329667616.1657663331&_gac=1.215513701.1673370037.Cj0KCQiAtvSdBhD0ARIsAPf8oNmXhRKk5wg6hR_Gk-OaIFNAHIAHK5RR4C15Ed8-JQHn7Ec-nPtGWO4aAmI7EALw_wcB)** .

Zscaler is thrilled to announce the **[Zscaler Zero Trust Certified Architect (ZTCA)](https://www.zscaler.com/ztca-zero-trust-certified-architect?_ga=2.20363576.1874724630.1673964266-1530160698.1673270731&_gl=1*sxk0bb*_ga*MTUzMDE2MDY5OC4xNjczMjcwNzMx*_ga_10SPJ4YJL9*MTY3NDA2Mjg0My4zMi4xLjE2NzQwNjI4NzAuMzMuMC4w)** certification, the industry’s first comprehensive Zero Trust certification. The first 25 folks to drop us a comment on our Community post will get the special discount code to get your certification! Become a Zero Trust Architect today!

**[The Seven Elements of Highly Successful Zero Trust Architecture](https://www.zscaler.com/resources/ebooks/seven-elements-of-highly-successful-zta.pdf?_gl=1*o25ref*_ga*ODE3NTA3NjMuMTY1NjAyMjQ0MA..*_ga_10SPJ4YJL9*MTY1NzY1OTMzMy4zMC4xLjE2NTc2NjE0MTEuMjM.&_ga=2.249337415.1358504439.1657642595-81750763.1656022440&_gac=1.19530058.1656443033.CjwKCAjwzeqVBhAoEiwAOrEmzdUaQeyDVi2fzcTHXzXFqTGe1eKCF6tEQwziid1-djW5ucwYT_kj6RoCK1QQAvD_BwE)** walks you through how to build your own security architecture by unlocking the power of the world’s largest security cloud to meet zero trust initiatives. If you’re a network and security professional that oversees zero-trust initiatives, this needs to be on your reading short list.

## Technical Moderator and Knowledge Manager!

- Source: https://community.zscaler.com/s/question/0D54u00009evmkmCAA/technical-moderator-and-knowledge-manager
- Type: Q&A
- Posted: 2022-07-12T20:53:12.000Z
- Last activity: 2025-09-03T12:05:09.000Z
- Answers: 8
- Likes: 3
- Note: ユーザー投稿であり Zscaler の公式見解ではない。内容が古い場合があるため投稿日を確認すること。
- Note: 回答本文は UI API では取得できないため未収録。

Hello Zenith Community!

My name is Ben Garrison, I would like to introduce myself as the Technical Moderator and Knowledge Manager. I am currently located in Tampa, FL and can’t wait to get more involved with this amazing community. I started last week and have been lurking the board and I am very excited to meet with you all.

Fun facts about me - I am really into Hockey (Colorado Avalanche), Football (Denver Broncos), and Dungeons and Dragons (Player and Game Master). I was in the Colorado National Guard for 15 years and served two tours of duty to Iraq, Turkey and Syria. I have been in IT for several years, and have a wealth of Community experience.

I am looking forward to assisting [@vcotton17](https://community.zscaler.com/u/vcotton17) in our community strategy and helping grow this community. The areas that I will mainly focus on, is our moderation and content strategy. This is important to ensure that we are tagging and capturing valuable content and information and surfacing it effectively.

Learning and becoming certified in the Zscaler products is top of my list of items to tackle. So I am sure that I will have tons of questions, and what better place to ask than here!

Don’t hesitate to reach out if you have any questions or need anything.

Best,
 Ben Garrison
<!-- /ZS-POST -->

Hello, I was thinking about a problem recently encountered with geofencing. There are web resources in countries that restrict access based on your IP addresses and drops the traffic if it is not within the country. Instead of trying to bypass every one of these things is there a way to write a statement in the PAC file that can identify wether or not a website is for fenced and then disable it if it is?

Do web servers have anything you can pull from a query that may identify it as being geo fenced? Looking to find a creative but not to difficult way around this. Any ideas are welcome.

As promised, here are some highlights from the recently concluded **Community Pulse Survey**. We want to be transparent about the health of our community and would love to encourage increased participation. We received many responses to our survey and some really great feedback and actionable insights.

I want you all to know that we are listening to you! You are heard and soon you will see improvements on our Community to make this an even better experience.

If you have any additional feedback, please leave me a comment on this post or DM me.

**Survey Participants:**
 48% - Customers | 38% - Partners | 14% - Employees

**Level of Community Experience:**
 42% - Very experienced | 34% - Somewhat experienced | 14% - Newbies | 10% - Leaders

**What brings you to Zenith Community:** (Clearly, some folks had a great sense of humor - see last line here)

## How To Series: Mark a correct response to your post as an "accepted solution"

- Source: https://community.zscaler.com/s/question/0D54u00009evmlsCAA/how-to-series-mark-a-correct-response-to-your-post-as-an-accepted-solution
- Type: Q&A
- Posted: 2022-05-31T18:20:08.000Z
- Last activity: 2023-07-06T12:57:16.000Z
- Answers: 1
- Likes: 1
- Note: ユーザー投稿であり Zscaler の公式見解ではない。内容が古い場合があるため投稿日を確認すること。
- Note: 回答本文は UI API では取得できないため未収録。

Accepted Solutions are a fabulous way to help our community members with the answers they need. It also recognizes the active and diligent members who invested their time in responding to the post.

Accepting a solution is very easy on our platform. Here is a step-by-step tutorial on how you can accept the right answers to your questions as solutions.

*NOTE: Only Authors of the posts and moderators/admins can accept responses as solutions.*

**Step 1 - Click on your post/topic**

[[Screen Shot 2022-05-31 at 11.17.33 AM] Screen Shot 2022-05-31 at 11.17.33 AM785×642 83.9 KB](https://community.zscaler.com/zenith/servlet/servlet.FileDownload?file=0154u000009O5WEAA0)

**Step 2 - Scroll down to the correct response that helped resolve your post/question**

[[Screen Shot 2022-05-31 at 11.17.33 AM] Screen Shot 2022-05-31 at 11.17.33 AM785×642 84.3 KB](https://community.zscaler.com/zenith/servlet/servlet.FileDownload?file=0154u000009O83iAAC)

**Step 3 - Click on the three dots in the bottom of the response, it will open the panel. Click on the Solution check box**

[[Screen Shot 2022-05-31 at 11.04.49 AM] Screen Shot 2022-05-31 at 11.04.49 AM782×470 65.8 KB](https://community.zscaler.com/zenith/servlet/servlet.FileDownload?file=0154u000009O7LMAA0)

**Step 4 - Refresh the page, you will now see the highlighted accepted solution immediately below your post**

[[Screen Shot 2022-05-31 at 11.05.22 AM] Screen Shot 2022-05-31 at 11.05.22 AM884×767 126 KB](https://community.zscaler.com/zenith/servlet/servlet.FileDownload?file=0154u000009O6jjAAC)

Please try to adopt the good habit of accepting solutions on your questions - this helps the users find answers and increases site credibility for all users.

If you have any other questions about this topic please don’t hesitate to reply to this post or reach out to me via DM.
 Good luck!
<!-- /ZS-POST -->

## Community Pulse Survey is now closed!

- Source: https://community.zscaler.com/s/question/0D54u00009evmm6CAA/community-pulse-survey-is-now-closed
- Type: Q&A
- Posted: 2022-05-16T21:00:53.000Z
- Last activity: 2023-05-31T09:08:43.000Z
- Answers: 4
- Likes: 2

- Note: 回答本文は UI API では取得できないため未収録。

**The Community Pulse Survey is now closed. Thank you for your participation!**

We are excited to gather feedback and experiences from our customers, partners, and all users to ensure that your voices impact the effectiveness of our community. I have met a lot of our external and internal influencers in the past few weeks and have gained priceless insights on their experiences. It is now time for me to hear from you!!

A few things about completing our survey:

- The survey can be taken by registered and non- registered users. We want to hear from all users!
- We keep individual opinions anonymous and only Community Staff will be reviewing the survey responses.
- When the survey closes, these insights will be presented on our community.
- Please provide as much information about your experiences as possible.
- Your Community username will be used to award you a cool *Survey Participation badge*!
- Your email address will only be used if you choose to be contacted by us.

We hope that by providing this quick and simple input process, we will hear from more users on our community, and from folks of different backgrounds and experiences.

You can participate in our [Community Pulse Survey](https://docs.google.com/forms/d/e/1FAIpQLSecBKg08BxjRZ19eeoanMl6n130vzByu5DX616hZW_03bJoAg/viewform) now!

Thank you for your participation!
<!-- /ZS-POST -->

Hello and Welcome to the Zenith Community!

There is nothing more amazing than people coming together for a shared purpose on a community. We are so glad you are here!

We are sure you have started looking around and have found a lot of topics of interest to you. We have a myriad of categories where you can consume content and post your own content. We encourage you to share your knowledge, ask questions, start new topics, engage, and become an integral part of this community.

Here are some additional resources available to you:

[Status of Zscaler Services](https://trust.zscaler.com)
 [How to locate the Hostnames and IP Addresses for ZIA Public Service Edges](https://config.zscaler.com/)

If you have any community related questions, you will find a lot of great content in the [Welcome to Zscaler Community](https://community.zscaler.com/c/welcome-to-the-zscaler-community/36) forum. There are many new topics that will appear in the "Latest Post’ column on the right side of the home page.

Always be sure to check out our Featured topics on the homepage banner.

[[image] image1101×408 124 KB](https://community.zscaler.com/zenith/servlet/servlet.FileDownload?file=0154u000009O8V8AAK)

We look forward to your continued participation!

## Hello! New Community Manager here!

- Source: https://community.zscaler.com/s/question/0D54u00009evmnQCAQ/hello-new-community-manager-here
- Type: Q&A
- Posted: 2022-03-24T21:56:41.000Z
- Last activity: 2023-07-06T12:59:33.000Z
- Answers: 9
- Likes: 9
- Note: ユーザー投稿であり Zscaler の公式見解ではない。内容が古い場合があるため投稿日を確認すること。
- Note: 回答本文は UI API では取得できないため未収録。

Hello Zenith Community!

My name is Vanitha and I would like to introduce myself as your new Community Manager. I am located in San Jose, California, and am so excited to meet and get to know **YOU**!

I have over 10 years of core community management experience and my focus and interest lies in creating and managing intelligent and usable online digital communities with supreme customer experience.

Fun facts about me - I love wine and reading, in that order. I am a very outdoorsy person and I love to entertain and have quality friends that I can cherish. I am also a total goof ball.

I am here to ***grow our community and help facilitate meaningful connections*** between the members. I want our amazing community to be your go-to knowledge-base resource for all your questions and concerns.

As a conversation starter, what do you like about our Zenith Community that keeps you coming back? How can I help you on your journey to be successful here and build your network?

In the coming days and weeks, please look for my posts and subscribe to them as I will be posting some community how-tos to learn, engage, and build your personal brand in the community.

I am looking forward to increased engagement and lots of conversations here. I am here to do everything I can, so please do not hesitate to reach out to me directly using your **community inbox** or by **messaging me** directly.

Best,
 Vanitha
<!-- /ZS-POST -->

<!-- ZS-POST {"url":"https://community.zscaler.com/s/question/0D54u00009evmnmCAA/china-mainland-and-windows-wwwmsftconnecttestcom","lastmod":"2023-05-31T08:46:13.000Z","id":"0D54u00009evmnmCAA"} -->
## China Mainland and Windows' www.msftconnecttest.com

- Source: https://community.zscaler.com/s/question/0D54u00009evmnmCAA/china-mainland-and-windows-wwwmsftconnecttestcom
- Type: Q&A
- Posted: 2022-03-02T10:12:17.000Z
- Last activity: 2023-05-31T08:46:13.000Z
- Answers: 5
- Likes: 0
- Note: ユーザー投稿であり Zscaler の公式見解ではない。内容が古い場合があるため投稿日を確認すること。
- Note: 回答本文は UI API では取得できないため未収録。

Hi all,

Does other Zscaler Customers suffer from same challenges as we are, when using Zscaler’s DCs inside mainland China?

We face problems with connecting to below URL that Microsoft use in Windows 10’s Network Location Awareness to detect if Internet is reachable, and many other Microsoft desktop products rely on Windows’ detection.

URL
 [http://www.msftconnecttest.com/connecttest.txt](http://www.msftconnecttest.com/connecttest.txt)

Microsoft also use AKAMAI Content Delivery Network for above URL, and it appears that the Chinese Government is blocking access to many of above hostname’s IP addresses at AKAMAI.

You can easily find them by using Web Insights with a filter like:

URL Search.Host = [www.msftconnecttest.com](http://www.msftconnecttest.com)
 Location = select relevant
 Received Bytes = Custom, min=0 bytes and max=1 byte.

You may find many hits with high count of milliseconds in column [Server Trans. Time (ms]" indicating time out.

Example of many using cURL via Tianjin:

C:\NetworkTest>curl --verbose [http://www.msftconnecttest.com/connecttest.txt](http://www.msftconnecttest.com/connecttest.txt)

- Trying 23.206.238.56:80…
- TCP_NODELAY set
- Connected to [www.msftconnecttest.com](http://www.msftconnecttest.com) (23.206.238.56) port 80 (#0)

> GET /connecttest.txt HTTP/1.1
 Host: [www.msftconnecttest.com](http://www.msftconnecttest.com)
 User-Agent: curl/7.67.0
 Accept: */*
>

- Mark bundle as not supporting multiuse
- HTTP 1.0, assume close after body < HTTP/1.0 504 Gateway Timeout < Server: Zscaler/6.1 < Content-Type: text/html < Connection: close <

504 Gateway Timeout
### Gateway Timeout

Server error - server 23.206.238.56 is unreachable at this moment.

Please retry the request or contact your administrator.

* Closing connection 0
Thank you,
 /Jesper
<!-- /ZS-POST -->

---

Hello, we are developing software which communicates over network protocols from client to server and from server to client.

For direct client request and server response this works:
 The client can reach the server with requests and based on that request the server can respond.

The server also pushes data to the client which fails:
 The server has to know the ip of the client in order to be able to push data to the client directly.
 This ip has to be configured in the software of the server application.
 A few months ago we used the “broker ip? from the zscaler app and it worked for a while.

Currently the pushed data from server to client through the broker ip does not work.
 The broker ip can be pinged but the client does not receive the data from the broker.
 I currently do not know how to achieve this. Any ideas?

Thank you and best regards Brayan

Now, for SOME users (which I can’t figure out a common denominator for) connect to Zscaler when working remotely and can’t see ANYTHING on the network. Before the parent company (which provides terrible support if any for us when we try to escalate things) disabled the old VPN, we would connect to it on a remote user’s PC and see the shared drive for instance. We’d switch to zscaler after disconnecting and closing out of old VPN and suddenly, no matter HOW you tried to reach it (ping, searching in FE, searching by FQDN in FE etc etc etc etc etc etc etc) NOTHING!!! YES I HAVE RESTARTED A BILLION TIMES. With no support for us local admins and little info online, please someone HELP ME. BECAUSE I GET BLAMED, NOT ZSCALER AND NOT THIS AWFUL PARENT COMPANY. PLEASE HELP.

## ASA to Zscaler IKEv2 Tunnel with NAT-T enabled

- Source: https://community.zscaler.com/s/question/0D54u00009evmr6CAA/asa-to-zscaler-ikev2-tunnel-with-natt-enabled
- Type: Q&A
- Posted: 2021-08-05T15:16:42.000Z
- Last activity: 2023-07-06T11:45:30.000Z
- Answers: 2
- Likes: 3
- Note: ユーザー投稿であり Zscaler の公式見解ではない。内容が古い場合があるため投稿日を確認すること。
- Note: 回答本文は UI API では取得できないため未収録。

This article talks about how to establish IKEv2 tunnel from ASA to Zscaler using Ikev2. When NAT is in place ASA starts sending its ike-id as private IP address which Zscaler cannot understand. ASA doesn’t have an option to set IKE-ID as FQDN, setting up IKE-ID as key-id doesn’t work with Zscaler because Zscaler doesn’t identify key-id as FQDN.

To solve this problem we can create VPN credentials using the private IP address of the ASA

[[image] image1206×206 18.8 KB](https://community.zscaler.com/zenith/servlet/servlet.FileDownload?file=0154u000009O4n8AAC)

In the screenshot above 10.96.19.202 is the private IP address of the ASA notice that the authentication type this time is IP address and not FQDN

We can then create a location and called the VPN credentials and Static IP address 10.96.19.202 (because it is static VPN Tunnel). Note that you can have multiple Static IP address on a Location so If you want to tie Public IP to a location along with the private IP address that will work too. Refer to the screenshot below I have a public as well as a private IP Space in Static IP address section.

[image]

On the ASA we can set the identity to AUTO and let ASA use IKE-ID as its private IP address. With that configuration in place we can build a tunnel to Zscaler

[[image] image972×395 13.4 KB](https://community.zscaler.com/zenith/servlet/servlet.FileDownload?file=0154u000009O5XDAA0)
<!-- /ZS-POST -->

---

<!-- ZS-POST {"url":"https://community.zscaler.com/s/question/0D54u00009evmsjCAA/community-reorganization","lastmod":"2023-05-31T09:07:54.000Z","id":"0D54u00009evmsjCAA"} -->
## Community Reorganization

- Source: https://community.zscaler.com/s/question/0D54u00009evmsjCAA/community-reorganization
- Type: Q&A
- Posted: 2021-03-23T20:47:59.000Z
- Last activity: 2023-05-31T09:07:54.000Z
- Answers: 0
- Likes: 1
- Note: ユーザー投稿であり Zscaler の公式見解ではない。内容が古い場合があるため投稿日を確認すること。
- Note: 回答本文は UI API では取得できないため未収録。

Hello all,

Just as Zscaler’s service offerings have grown and expanded over the years, the community needs to do the same.

Starting Friday afternoon, we’ll be doing some renaming and moving of categories, as well as adding some new ones. The products category will be replaced with a number of top-level categories built around our services and solutions. This will better align our community to how our internal teams are organized.

While we’ll try to make this as smooth as possible, there will be some bumps for users who’ve been around a while:

- Email filters will break – Subject lines will change with the category name change.
- Some categories won’t have a 1-1 mapping from the old to the new, so please read the descriptions next week. I’ll update this post with the final mappings.

We’re looking forward to making the community more robust and getting answers to your questions.

Thanks!
 [@awl](https://community.zscaler.com/u/awl)
<!-- /ZS-POST -->

---

## Securing AnyDesk/Teamviewer

- Source: https://community.zscaler.com/s/question/0D54u00009evmtTCAQ/securing-anydeskteamviewer
- Type: Q&A
- Posted: 2021-02-18T11:33:24.000Z
- Last activity: 2023-05-31T08:13:54.000Z
- Answers: 0
- Likes: 0
- Note: ユーザー投稿であり Zscaler の公式見解ではない。内容が古い場合があるため投稿日を確認すること。
- Note: 回答本文は UI API では取得できないため未収録。

Hello community,

for a customer project we need to implement/allow AnyDesk/Teamviewer for some of our employees. Does anyone have experience and/or best practice recommendations how to secure this kind of remote management software with Zscaler (besides of application settings like MFA, turning on/off particular security settings etc). Especially the default enabled “features? of AnyDesk are - to put it positively - quite comprehensive.

I am thinking of somehow completly blocking INBOUND traffic to these applications via Zscaler/-Z-App, but I fear this is no easy task as AnyDesk/TV are basically working like ZIA/ZPA where only outbound connections are initiated and both ends are interconnected.

Another idea (for the time being my favorite) would be to completely block installation of that stuff on our client-machines and implement some kind of isolated “jumpserver/bastion?-host from where this connections are made.

Any experience/recommendations/advise would be helpful so i don’t have to invent the wheel twice.

BR
 Manuel
<!-- /ZS-POST -->

<!-- ZS-POST {"url":"https://community.zscaler.com/s/question/0D54u00009evmuVCAQ/new-users-and-sending-private-messages","lastmod":"2023-07-06T11:45:59.000Z","id":"0D54u00009evmuVCAQ"} -->
## New users and sending private messages

- Source: https://community.zscaler.com/s/question/0D54u00009evmuVCAQ/new-users-and-sending-private-messages
- Type: Q&A
- Posted: 2021-01-08T22:12:53.000Z
- Last activity: 2023-07-06T11:45:59.000Z
- Answers: 0
- Likes: 2
- Note: ユーザー投稿であり Zscaler の公式見解ではない。内容が古い場合があるため投稿日を確認すること。
- Note: 回答本文は UI API では取得できないため未収録。

### Trust Levels and Private Messages

The Zenith Community uses a system of Trust Levels that provide additional functionality in the community. One of these features is the ability to send private messages.

When you first join you are at Trust Level 0. As you read posts your time, topic, and post counts accumulate. You will be automatically promoted to Trust Level 1 and gain the ability to send private messages once you’ve done enough reading.

You can get to Trust Level 1 by:

- Entering at least **5** topics
- Reading at least **30** posts
- Spend a total of **10** minutes reading posts

### Reading Private Messages

The messages functionality is contained in your user profile. If you have a private message waiting you will receive a notification bubble on your avatar.

[Screen Shot 2021-01-11 at 5.08.35 PM]

To access your messages click your avatar and then the envelope icon.

[Screen Shot 2021-01-08 at 1.56.39 PM]

### Starting a new message

Click on the envelope icon above a second time and you will be taken to your inbox. Use the new message button on that screen to compose a new message. You can add more than one user if needed, but you need to know their user names.

[Screen Shot 2021-01-08 at 1.56.56 PM]
<!-- /ZS-POST -->

Hello,
 Since the EST morning of 2020-11-30, some of our tools fail to accept the Zscaler-rewritten cert chains. Using the “openssl s_client -connect SITE:443 -showcerts?, I could see that the self-signed Zscaler Root CA was missing from the chain (i.e. the chain started with a Zscaler intermediate cert signed by the Zscaler Root CA). I filed a support case 02644981 but received no confirmation (I was not even registered).

This is urgent as we have many builds using an external SaaS site for component analysis.

Hello Everyone!

We have rolled out the ZIA App to our organization - but we have felt some issues with the o365 One Click - The main bug being intermittent silent calls when using MS Teams Enterprise Voice. As a test, we assigned some test users their own app profile, and bypassed everything Microsoft related from [https://docs.microsoft.com/en-us/microsoft-365/enterprise/urls-and-ip-address-ranges?view=o365-worldwide](https://docs.microsoft.com/en-us/microsoft-365/enterprise/urls-and-ip-address-ranges?view=o365-worldwide)

This has had a positive affect and vastly reduced the silent calls.

Mgmt have decided they want to roll out o365 bypassing to everyone - is there a clean or recommended way to achieve this? An api maybe?

Many Thanks,
 Chris
<!-- /ZS-POST -->

Hello All, I have a constant issue with zscaler app and office365 applications. Currently, I can see outlook stops updating when I enable my vpn tunnel client.

- We are using mix of windows and mac os. Therefore, 2 profiles
- All O365 are whitelisted in pac files, no blocking is taking place except ‘Gambling’ sites.
- VPN client / server is pritunl which operates in full tunnel mode
- I have attached the screenshots for both profiles.
- Zscaler support previously suggested to turn off filtering when VPN is enabled but that did not worked out as Zscaler does not recognises pritunl client interfaces. I would rather not do this at all as most of our users are on vpn all the time.

Hi ,

 15 45 ms 45 ms 44 ms [ae-12.r03.tkokhk01.hk.bb.gin.ntt.net](http://ae-12.r03.tkokhk01.hk.bb.gin.ntt.net) [129.250.5.254]
 16 51 ms 53 ms 53 ms [ae-11.r24.tkokhk01.hk.bb.gin.ntt.net](http://ae-11.r24.tkokhk01.hk.bb.gin.ntt.net) [129.250.6.99]
 17 45 ms 44 ms 44 ms [ae-6.r20.sngpsi07.sg.bb.gin.ntt.net](http://ae-6.r20.sngpsi07.sg.bb.gin.ntt.net) [129.250.7.66]

<!-- ZS-POST {"url":"https://community.zscaler.com/s/question/0D54u00009evmzcCAA/arm64-surface-pro-x-support-need-arm64compiled-network-adapter-driver","lastmod":"2023-07-06T13:13:25.000Z","id":"0D54u00009evmzcCAA"} -->
## ARM64 / Surface Pro X Support? Need ARM64-compiled Network Adapter Driver

- Source: https://community.zscaler.com/s/question/0D54u00009evmzcCAA/arm64-surface-pro-x-support-need-arm64compiled-network-adapter-driver
- Type: Q&A
- Posted: 2020-02-24T07:47:59.000Z
- Last activity: 2023-07-06T13:13:25.000Z
- Answers: 18
- Likes: 1
- Note: ユーザー投稿であり Zscaler の公式見解ではない。内容が古い場合があるため投稿日を確認すること。
- Note: 回答本文は UI API では取得できないため未収録。

Are there plans to release a version of the Zscaler app that will install and run properly on Surface Pro X?

While that app itself is x86/win32 and installs ok, the Zscaler Network Adapter Driver is the problem. the TAP driver install at the end of the app installation process fails.

So, you can complete the installer routine on HP Envy X2 (snapdragon), Surface Pro X, Galaxy Book S, and Zscaler can be logged in/configured as normal, it will find the native AD rules etc as normal, Internet Security fires up and runs as normal - HOWEVER Private Access can not start because it gets a driver error due to the failure to install the Network Adapter Driver.

ARM64 Windows requires an ARM64-compiled driver.

Based on what MSFT posted here, [https://docs.microsoft.com/en-us/windows-hardware/drivers/develop/building-arm64-drivers](https://docs.microsoft.com/en-us/windows-hardware/drivers/develop/building-arm64-drivers) ,

Recompiling the existing Win32/Win64 driver for ARM64 would take mere minutes, if not seconds.

Therefore, it is easily possible either release a newer ZScaler installer that is able to recognize the ARM64 environment and install the proper, newly-included ARM64 version of the driver, OR to simply release the driver fileset itself without worrying about the rest of the installer - it can be remediated by a separate install of the driver via the INF file, to get Zscaler up and running.

Just suggestions. There are multiple models in device release pipelines for always-on ARM64 Windows machines now that Microsoft and Samsung have openly collaborated with Qualcomm and validated the platform, so hopefully Zscaler will keep up with Microsoft hardware at least. THanks.
<!-- /ZS-POST -->

---

## Newly Registered Domain block enforcement

- Source: https://community.zscaler.com/s/question/0D54u00009evn1GCAQ/newly-registered-domain-block-enforcement
- Type: Q&A
- Posted: 2019-09-24T13:05:32.000Z
- Last activity: 2023-05-31T08:51:38.000Z
- Answers: 1
- Likes: 0
- Note: ユーザー投稿であり Zscaler の公式見解ではない。内容が古い場合があるため投稿日を確認すること。
- Note: 回答本文は UI API では取得できないため未収録。

Like everyone else, we’ve been seeing a significant number of phishing campaigns utilizing new domain registration to try and avoid detection. We have Zscaler’s “Enable Newly Registered Domain Lookup? turned on and the “Newly Registered Domains? URL category set to “block?. Unfortunately, we’re repeatedly seeing in logs that the first user that visits a “Newly Registered Domain? is initially allowed access before eventually being blocked. Obviously this isn’t ideal. We opened a ticket and were told this was a bug at first, but now we’ve been instructed we’d have to make a “feature request? to get this fixed. We never had this issue with our previous solution which also had “newly registered domain? block functionality. Are we missing something?
<!-- /ZS-POST -->

<!-- ZS-POST {"url":"https://community.zscaler.com/s/question/0D54u00009evn1JCAQ/readonly-access-to-github-and-alternative-sites","lastmod":"2023-05-31T09:24:38.000Z","id":"0D54u00009evn1JCAQ"} -->
## Read-only access to github and alternative sites

- Source: https://community.zscaler.com/s/question/0D54u00009evn1JCAQ/readonly-access-to-github-and-alternative-sites
- Type: Q&A
- Posted: 2019-09-20T18:43:26.000Z
- Last activity: 2023-05-31T09:24:38.000Z
- Answers: 6
- Likes: 1
- Note: ユーザー投稿であり Zscaler の公式見解ではない。内容が古い場合があるため投稿日を確認すること。
- Note: 回答本文は UI API では取得できないため未収録。

We are exploring how we might permit read-only access to [github.com](http://github.com) and [github.io](http://github.io) for our end users. The application control currently only has options to block or allow, so that doesn’t help (although we have asked our TAM to open an enhancement request for that control to include a read-only option in future). We are decrypting the traffic to these sites, so we are testing now to try and identify keywords/strings in the URLs to effectively block certain functions on the site like upload or post (to prevent data leakage). We have had success doing this with youtube and some other streaming sites. Has anyone else already explored this with github and do you have any keywords/strings that you could share for blocking functionality on the site? Also, what alternatives to github should we also consider making read-only to prevent data leakage? Thank you!
<!-- /ZS-POST -->

---

<!-- ZS-POST {"url":"https://community.zscaler.com/s/question/0D54u00009evn3kCAA/additional-functionality-for-the-community","lastmod":"2023-07-06T11:47:07.000Z","id":"0D54u00009evn3kCAA"} -->
## Additional functionality for the community

- Source: https://community.zscaler.com/s/question/0D54u00009evn3kCAA/additional-functionality-for-the-community
- Type: Q&A
- Posted: 2018-10-12T23:05:53.000Z
- Last activity: 2023-07-06T11:47:07.000Z
- Answers: 0
- Likes: 0
- Note: ユーザー投稿であり Zscaler の公式見解ではない。内容が古い場合があるため投稿日を確認すること。
- Note: 回答本文は UI API では取得できないため未収録。

Hello all,

We are alwyas looking at ways to enhance the funtionality of the community, and with that we’ve added a few components.

**Dark Theme**

You may have already seen the dark theme is available. Now if you’d like to switch between themes during the day and night you can do so via the Hamburger Menu.

[dark-light-theme-selector]

**Social Sharing**

Find a post you’d like to share? You can post it directly to LinkedIn, Twitter, or Facebook via the link button.

[community-social-share]

**Copy Code Blocks**

If you see a code block on the site there will now be a small copy button in the upper right hand corner that will copy the contents of that code block to your clipboard.

To create a code block just indent your lines with 4 spaces. Check out the [Commonmark (Markdown)](https://community.zscaler.com/t/markdown-commonmark-editing-syntax/2132) tutorial for more info.

**Auto Linking of Words**

Certain key words in your post will now automatically turn into links. These will typically go to tag pages, effectively building in a meta-search to your post, or a Zscaler external resource.
<!-- /ZS-POST -->

---

Our Company just started to use Zscaler Business Bundle.
 It is managed by an external company.

Yesterday my office was connected through Zscaler and almost directly we
 realized that we couldn’t use ftp? Whenever we try to connect to a remote
 site we get error: 403 Access not allowed by your Company policy

We contacted the company that manages our Zscaler settings and they told us
 that they have enabled FTP over HTTP and also native FTP so the problem is
 not in Zscaler.

Is that really true though? Can’t it be that some firewall or other policy set
 in Zscaler is what’s stopping us?

Just trying to understand how this works and rule out things.

BR
 Rob

<!-- ZS-POST {"url":"https://community.zscaler.com/s/question/0D54u00009evn48CAA/dark-theme-available","lastmod":"2023-07-06T13:15:15.000Z","id":"0D54u00009evn48CAA"} -->
## Dark Theme Available

- Source: https://community.zscaler.com/s/question/0D54u00009evn48CAA/dark-theme-available
- Type: Q&A
- Posted: 2018-08-29T17:01:18.000Z
- Last activity: 2023-07-06T13:15:15.000Z
- Answers: 2
- Likes: 6
- Note: ユーザー投稿であり Zscaler の公式見解ではない。内容が古い場合があるため投稿日を確認すること。
- Note: 回答本文は UI API では取得できないため未収録。

If you’d rather browse Zenith with a dark theme that’s now available in your user settings. To get started click your avatar in the upper right, then on the gear. On the preferences tab go down to interface on the left side. Here you can switch between dark and light themes.

[[theme-change] theme-change.png1209×583 49.5 KB](https://community.zscaler.com/zenith/servlet/servlet.FileDownload?file=0154u000009O7qPAAS)

Once you’ve changed your theme make sure you hit save at the bottom of the screen.

NOTE: You will need to do a full page reload to get the banner image to update.

[[after] after.png1114×615 286 KB](https://community.zscaler.com/zenith/servlet/servlet.FileDownload?file=0154u000009O8BnAAK)

We’re still playing with the colors a bit, so feel free to reach out if something doesn’t look right. You can switch back anytime by following the same process and selecting the light theme.
<!-- /ZS-POST -->

---

## Behavior of Time Interval rules for traffic from location and for road-warriors

- Source: https://community.zscaler.com/s/question/0D54u00009evn57CAA/behavior-of-time-interval-rules-for-traffic-from-location-and-for-roadwarriors
- Type: Q&A

- Note: 回答本文は UI API では取得できないため未収録。

**Setup:**

- **Time Interval:** [[image] image599×773 21.6 KB](https://community.zscaler.com/zenith/servlet/servlet.FileDownload?file=0154u000009O5ZWAA0)
- **Policy:** [[image] image1616×150 10.9 KB](https://community.zscaler.com/zenith/servlet/servlet.FileDownload?file=0154u000009O5eXAAS)

**Scenario 1: Traffic sent from a defined location**

- **DPP Location:** [[image] image921×618 26.7 KB](https://community.zscaler.com/zenith/servlet/servlet.FileDownload?file=0154u000009O4wPAAS)
- **Time on node:** [image]
- Time in Europe/London: **13:17 PM**

**Traffic blocked as per time based rule:**

[[image] image893×408 16.9 KB](https://community.zscaler.com/zenith/servlet/servlet.FileDownload?file=0154u000009O56QAAS)

**Outcome: Policy applies as per time zone defined on the location.**

**Scenario 2: Traffic coming from a roadwarrior (not from a defined location):**

- **Time on node:** [image]
- **Traffic allowed:** [[image] image1882×761 104 KB](https://community.zscaler.com/zenith/servlet/servlet.FileDownload?file=0154u000009O5MLAA0)

**Outcome: Policy applies as per node time zone.**

<!-- ZS-POST {"url":"https://community.zscaler.com/s/question/0D54u00009evn5FCAQ/markdown-commonmark-editing-syntax","lastmod":"2023-05-31T09:07:59.000Z","id":"0D54u00009evn5FCAQ"} -->
## Markdown / Commonmark Editing Syntax

- Source: https://community.zscaler.com/s/question/0D54u00009evn5FCAQ/markdown-commonmark-editing-syntax
- Type: Q&A
- Posted: 2018-03-07T19:28:17.000Z
- Last activity: 2023-05-31T09:07:59.000Z
- Answers: 0
- Likes: 1
- Note: ユーザー投稿であり Zscaler の公式見解ではない。内容が古い場合があるため投稿日を確認すること。
- Note: 回答本文は UI API では取得できないため未収録。

As you start editing on the community if you are familiar with Markdown formatting lanugage you’ll find you can use those formatting tools on the community. This system allows you to write in plain text with simple markup language that is converted to HTML once you have completed editing.

The editor supports the [Commonmark](http://commonmark.org/) version of Markdown, which is an effort to standardize Markdown.

For a quick cheat sheet reference please see [http://commonmark.org/help/](http://commonmark.org/help/). If you aren’t yet familiar with markdown there is a 10 minute tutorial on the help page to get you started.
<!-- /ZS-POST -->

---

<!-- ZS-POST {"url":"https://community.zscaler.com/s/question/0D54u00009evn5GCAQ/using-bookmarks-to-find-your-way-back-to-topics","lastmod":"2023-07-06T11:47:29.000Z","id":"0D54u00009evn5GCAQ"} -->
## Using Bookmarks to find your way back to topics

- Source: https://community.zscaler.com/s/question/0D54u00009evn5GCAQ/using-bookmarks-to-find-your-way-back-to-topics
- Type: Q&A
- Posted: 2018-03-05T22:26:33.000Z
- Last activity: 2023-07-06T11:47:29.000Z
- Answers: 0
- Likes: 0
- Note: ユーザー投稿であり Zscaler の公式見解ではない。内容が古い場合があるため投稿日を確認すること。
- Note: 回答本文は UI API では取得できないため未収録。

There are some topics that over time you will find useful to refer back to. In many cases search will be sufficient to get you back there, but there are cases where you likely would just want to jump back to the page. This is where community bookmarks can be useful. To use book marks simply click on the ellipsis button at the bottom of the post you want to bookmark:

[36%20PM]

Then click on the bookmark button:

[28%20PM]

To get back to your book marks simply click on your user name, then the bookmark flag and you’ll be taken to a list of your bookmarks:

[14%20PM]

[53%20PM]
<!-- /ZS-POST -->

---

<!-- ZS-POST {"url":"https://community.zscaler.com/s/question/0D54u00009evn5ICAQ/country-blocking","lastmod":"2025-08-22T07:27:43.000Z","id":"0D54u00009evn5ICAQ"} -->
## Country Blocking

- Source: https://community.zscaler.com/s/question/0D54u00009evn5ICAQ/country-blocking
- Type: Q&A
- Posted: 2018-02-20T22:36:05.000Z
- Last activity: 2025-08-22T07:27:43.000Z
- Answers: 14
- Likes: 1
- Note: ユーザー投稿であり Zscaler の公式見解ではない。内容が古い場合があるため投稿日を確認すること。
- Note: 回答本文は UI API では取得できないため未収録。

Curious if anyone has any experience or feedback on country based blocks. We are considering blocking traffic to Russia, China, Cuba, North Korea, etc. We don’t have offices in these countries. Has anyone else implemented this and if so do you have any best practices? Looking at our traffic over the past month, we definitely see a fair amount of transactions with China and Russia where the URL category is Internet Services so I’m concerned of any potential business impact due to any Internet infrastructure hosted there.

Follow up question. I’m doing my reporting in our SIEM but does Zscaler provide any native reporting on GEO IP?
<!-- /ZS-POST -->

Today we installed the saved searches module on Zenith. This module allows you to save terms you are interested in and be notified by private message when they occur in a topic.

You can access this under the preferences menu item.

[35 AM]

Next click the last menu item on the left to go to the saved searches dialog. Enter or modify your search terms there and click Save Changes.

Hello all,

Just a heads up that on Tuesday Sept 12 we will be doing a soft launch of the community. We will open up the public forums and start sending out the first wave of invites. This means over the next week I’ll be cleaning up posts and moving things to the public side of the community. I plan to open the forums up Tuesday morning once I’m sure things are settled.

Also of note for email users, the community’s name will officially change to Zenith this Friday evening PST. If you are using email filters to sort your messages from the community, please be advised that if you may need to adjust them if Z-Community is what you are filtering on.

Thanks,
 Andy
<!-- /ZS-POST -->

<!-- ZS-POST {"url":"https://community.zscaler.com/s/question/0D54u00009evn6PCAQ/keeping-your-information-safe-on-the-community","lastmod":"2023-05-31T08:14:53.000Z","id":"0D54u00009evn6PCAQ"} -->
## Keeping your information safe on the community

- Source: https://community.zscaler.com/s/question/0D54u00009evn6PCAQ/keeping-your-information-safe-on-the-community
- Type: Q&A
- Posted: 2017-05-09T20:39:27.000Z
- Last activity: 2023-05-31T08:14:53.000Z
- Answers: 0
- Likes: 0
- Note: ユーザー投稿であり Zscaler の公式見解ではない。内容が古い場合があるため投稿日を確認すること。
- Note: 回答本文は UI API では取得できないため未収録。

The Z-Community is here to help you with successfully deploy Zscaler services in your organization. From time-to-time you’re likely going to need to post data that concerns your specific deployment. To that end we’d like to suggest a few things to help keep your data and your organization’s information safe.

#### What do the little Zscaler logos on profile pictures mean?

If you see a small Zscaler Z logo on someone’s avatar that indicates they are a Zscaler employee. There title should show up too.

#### Sometimes less specific is better

As with any other public forum, you want to be careful when posting to the community anything that can be directly related back to your organization. It’s not a good idead to post your organization’s name, actual IP addressing, or overly specific network diagrams in an open forum.

- Because you’ll be asking questions about your organizations design and deployment, it’s best to keep your organization’s name off your posts.
- If you are asking a question about the specific industry you work in, it’s best to refer to the regulation you need help meeting.
- Consider using generic icons for network equipment in your diagrams, unless the speific equipment is the question.
- Where posible, use private IP addressing, espeically if your organization uses public IPs.
- Make sure you scrub the logs and console output for names and addressing.
- Screen shots should have any identifable information edited out.
- Consider leaving your real name off of your profile.
- Don’t use your company logo as your avatar.

#### Watch out for those email signatures

Many of you may interact with the community via email. Doing so is fully supported, but you need to be careful with the content. As this is a public forum it is quite easy for someone to view your contact info.

We use [Discourse](http://www.discourse.org/) as our community platform, and it supports Usenet signature removal. The patters, in plain text, is dash, dash, space on a line of it’s own such as:

```
-- [new line]
```

### Be sure to read the docs:

- [FAQ](https://community.zscaler.com/faq)
- [Terms of Service](https://community.zscaler.com/tos)
<!-- /ZS-POST -->

---

<!-- ZS-POST {"url":"https://community.zscaler.com/s/question/0D54u00009evn6RCAQ/configuring-email-notifications-on-the-community","lastmod":"2024-01-29T13:20:56.000Z","id":"0D54u00009evn6RCAQ"} -->
## Configuring email & notifications on the community

- Source: https://community.zscaler.com/s/question/0D54u00009evn6RCAQ/configuring-email-notifications-on-the-community
- Type: Q&A
- Posted: 2016-12-09T21:01:58.000Z
- Last activity: 2024-01-29T13:20:56.000Z
- Answers: 4
- Likes: 0
- Note: ユーザー投稿であり Zscaler の公式見解ではない。内容が古い場合があるため投稿日を確認すること。
- Note: 回答本文は UI API では取得できないため未収録。

One of the features we really like about using [Discourse](http://www.discourse.org/) as our community platform is the ability to turn on mailing list mode to receive emails about topics and categories. This gives you the ability to interact with discourse threads via email when you are on the go or simply prefer to work from your email client for responses.

If however you simply want to be notified about particular categories when you log in that can also be configured. This will notify you at different levels depending on what you select over categories.

#### Email Signatures

If you use a signature block with graphics, your phone number, or email, you will want to strip that out so that it doesn’t appear on the community. To do this you will need to follow the Usenet RFC standard for email signature dividers.

You signature needs to start with a line that contains two dashes and a space, followed by a return, in plain text. This should be placed on it’s own line before your signature block, the rest of the signature can still be HTML or rich text.

Be sure this is in a font such as Courier or similar to prevent rich text formatting of the dashes.

> – [new line]
 Your Signature
>

Your current signature goes below the line, and will be stripped if you email into the system. For more info please see section 4.3 of the RFC at [https://tools.ietf.org/html/rfc3676](https://tools.ietf.org/html/rfc3676).

#### Mailing List Mode

Please be aware that Mailing List Mode **subscribes you to all posts**. If that’s not what you want please scroll down to the notifications section.

To set up mailing list mode we first need to decide what volume of notification you want to receive. Start by clicking on your avatar in the upper right, and then on the gear below that.

[image]

Next, scroll about halfway down until you see mailing list mode. You’ll want to check that box then decide what level of interaction you want with the system.

[image]

#### Notifications on posts in certain topics or forums

If you want to be notified when on the site you will need to select to watch topics or forums, and tweak your email settings.

On each forum there is a pull down you can use to select the frequency of notifications. Select “Watching? from the drop down will enable you to receive emails for each post and is most likely what you want if you are covering a specific topic.

For instance, if you wanted to watch all of ZPA you would browse to that sub-forum under products. In the upper right below your avatar you’ll see a button with a circle in it. Click on this to select your drop down option for that thread or category.

[image]

Now you need to adjust your profile. The following settings will ensure you get every post as a mail even when you are on the site. The feel free to adjust the amount of quoting as you like:

[image]
<!-- /ZS-POST -->

## Using Tags in the community

- Source: https://community.zscaler.com/s/question/0D54u00009evnDxCAI/using-tags-in-the-community
- Type: Q&A
- Posted: 2021-07-08T19:22:46.000Z
- Last activity: 2023-07-07T11:21:20.000Z
- Answers: 0
- Likes: 1
- Note: ユーザー投稿であり Zscaler の公式見解ではない。内容が古い場合があるため投稿日を確認すること。
- Note: 回答本文は UI API では取得できないため未収録。

Tags on the community is a free form field and can be defined by the individual user. Tags act like a saved search, and you want to focus on the most relevant tag.

To get started you simply need to start entering text into the tag field next to the category, and below the title. A list of previously generated tags that match your string will start to appear. If no tag matches, you can create your own tag. See the example below:

[Screen Shot 2021-07-08 at 12.21.02 PM]

A couple things to remember:

- Tags can’t have spaces in them
- We’ve limited you five (5) tags per post
- Make sure your tag is broad enough to matter, we have a lot of tags with only one post
<!-- /ZS-POST -->

**UPDATED 6/1/2023: Please read follow on comments for the latest on the migration and Community Read-only status**

The Zenith Community will go into Read Only mode in order to facilitate the migration to our new community platform. This update will happen at 6:30 PM PST on 05/31 and will remain read-only for 24-48 hours.

During this time you will be able to continue to log in and read topics but you will not be able to create new posts or respond. Be on the lookout for an email from the community with updates and information on how to get started on our new platform once the migration is complete.

If you have any questions or need any help, please reach out to the community team at [community@zscaler.com](mailto:community@zscaler.com)

## How to log out and log in end users without requiring them to enter a password

- Note: 回答本文は UI API では取得できないため未収録。

Hi Zscaler & Community,

I have implemented changes that require users to completely exit Zscaler and log out, then back in, for the changes to take effect. The only issue is, when we disable/remove Zscaler from device on ZCC it requires the user to enter a password, as opposed to an auto-login which would be much preferred.

Users will refrain from logging in therefore creating more issues than solutions. Were there any workarounds/solutions that could achieve this? Thanks!

- Liam
<!-- /ZS-POST -->

<!-- ZS-POST {"url":"https://community.zscaler.com/s/question/0D54u00009eykPvCAI/internal-error-please-contact-administrator","lastmod":"2024-02-23T02:08:14.000Z","id":"0D54u00009eykPvCAI"} -->
## Internal Error Please Contact Administrator

- Source: https://community.zscaler.com/s/question/0D54u00009eykPvCAI/internal-error-please-contact-administrator
- Type: Q&A
- Posted: 2023-04-10T16:52:27.000Z
- Last activity: 2024-02-23T02:08:14.000Z
- Answers: 4
- Likes: 0
- Note: ユーザー投稿であり Zscaler の公式見解ではない。内容が古い場合があるため投稿日を確認すること。
- Note: 回答本文は UI API では取得できないため未収録。

Hello All, I have a user that has an HP Zbook 17 G8 and gets this error whenever he tries to log into Zscaler on it - Internal Error, Please Contact Administrator. Action: Ensure that the version of Zscaler Client Connector is current. Check that traffic is not being modified between the device and the server. - The options are Learn more and OK. I have uninstalled and reinstalled the latest version zscaler twice, making sure that I restart the computer before reinstalling zscaler again. I have run all HP driver updates and Windows updates as well. I had the user sign into zscaler on another computer and he had no issues, so it must be that computer itself. I could just reimage the users Zbook computer but the user has very expensive software on it that would require re-licensing that software again and that is very painful process. Does anyone know how I can fix this issue without re-imaging the computer? Any help would be appreciated, I thank you all very much.
<!-- /ZS-POST -->

Does ZScaler’s packet capture w/npcap alter the flow of network traffic to/from your system?

The reason why I ask is because our company is running into issues where Office365 is unable to reach the licensing server to reactivate the client and when starting packet capture with ZScaler the license goes through and Office activates itself (Office only looks for activation every 30 days), so this issue is being difficult to troubleshoot because our troubleshooting/investigation steps are resolving the issue and after 30 days the issue returns because packet capture isn’t continuously running.

Ps. This is only affecting Office products. Every other application/network path seems to be running fine. I would greatly appreciate any answers on this one.

## Connection Error when switching from Wireless to LAN

- Source: https://community.zscaler.com/s/question/0D54u00009eylMGCAY/connection-error-when-switching-from-wireless-to-lan
- Type: Q&A
- Posted: 2023-05-09T03:21:19.000Z
- Last activity: 2023-06-02T17:12:07.000Z
- Answers: 1
- Likes: 0
- Note: ユーザー投稿であり Zscaler の公式見解ではない。内容が古い場合があるため投稿日を確認すること。
- Note: 回答本文は UI API では取得できないため未収録。

Hi,
 We have several users where whenever they switch from WIRELESS to LAN, i.e plug into their docking station, Zscaler comes up with “Connection Error”. Users cannot access any internet sites or any applications that we have put a BYPASS in place for.

Usually a restart will fix the issue, however, its gotten to the point where some users it takes 5-6 reboots to fix the issue.

Ive seen it happen on 3.8.0.102, 4.0.0.80 & 4.0.0.89. We’ve also tired turning Zscaler OFF on some of these machine whilst troubleshooting, but the issue still occurs.

Thanks
<!-- /ZS-POST -->

<p><span style="font-family: Arial; font-size: 10.5pt;">On your profile dropdown, you will find some additional options that will allow you to view your notifications, your bookmarks, your inbox, and your profile and preferences.</span></p><p><span style="font-family: Arial; font-size: 10.5pt;">Here is what it looks like -</span></p><p><img src="/zenith/sfc/servlet.shepherd/version/download/0684u00000aBFLl?asPdf=false&amp;operationContext=CHATTER"></p><p><br></p><p><span style="font-family: Arial; font-size: 10.5pt;">The first icon on your left is the notifications bell icon. Here is where you will notice if you have received any notifications from your peers or from the Community team. When you log in, it is best practice to scan this area and ensure you have read all your notifications.</span></p><p><br></p><p><span style="font-family: Arial; font-size: 10.5pt;">In the dropdown, you will find the following:</span></p><ul><li><span style="font-family: Arial; font-size: 10.5pt;">Home : Takes you back to the Community homepage</span></li><li><span style="font-family: Arial; font-size: 10.5pt;">My Profile: You can view and edit your profile details</span></li><li><span style="font-family: Arial; font-size: 10.5pt;">My Settings: You can set your account and notification preferences here</span></li><li><span style="font-family: Arial; font-size: 10.5pt;">My Messages: You can see your sent, new, unread, and archived messages here</span></li><li><span style="font-family: Arial; font-size: 10.5pt;">Logout: This link logs you out of the Community</span></li></ul><p><br></p><p><span style="font-family: Arial; font-size: 10.5pt;">Be sure to check all these options out.</span></p><p><br></p><p><a href="https://community.zscaler.com/zenith/s/question/0D54u00009ezDdoCAE/step-9-report-inappropriate-content" rel="noopener noreferrer" target="_blank">UP NEXT - Report inappropriate content</a></p><p><br></p><p><br></p>
<!-- /ZS-POST -->

<!-- ZS-POST {"url":"https://community.zscaler.com/s/question/0D54u00009ezFsTCAU/step-7-how-to-become-a-trailblazer-influencer","lastmod":"2026-03-07T17:31:00.000Z","id":"0D54u00009ezFsTCAU"} -->
## STEP 7 - How to become a Trailblazer Influencer

- Source: https://community.zscaler.com/s/question/0D54u00009ezFsTCAU/step-7-how-to-become-a-trailblazer-influencer
- Type: Q&A
- Posted: 2023-06-03T18:05:58.000Z
- Last activity: 2026-03-07T17:31:00.000Z
- Answers: 0
- Likes: 3
- Note: ユーザー投稿であり Zscaler の公式見解ではない。内容が古い場合があるため投稿日を確認すること。
- Note: 回答本文は UI API では取得できないため未収録。

<p><span style="font-family: Arial; font-size: 10.5pt;">The Trailblazer Program elevates top contributing community members to engage in our Community to model successful behaviors, provide guidance, and inspire the next generation of Trailblazers.</span></p><p><br></p><p><span style="font-family: Arial; font-size: 10.5pt;">Membership in the Community Trailblazer Program is a leadership position and you should take immense pride in it. Primarily, it is important to remember that you are representing the community and assisting scale self service on the Community. As a member of the program, you will also receive many special rewards.</span></p><p><br></p><p><span style="font-family: Arial; font-size: 10.5pt;">So, what should you do to become a Trailblazer?</span></p><ul><li><span style="font-size: 10.5pt;">Visit the Community often</span></li><li><span style="font-size: 10.5pt;">Share your expertise</span></li><li><span style="font-size: 10.5pt;">Help other members</span></li><li><span style="font-size: 10.5pt;">Be a model citizen</span></li><li><span style="font-size: 10.5pt;">Contribute to topics and create new impactful content</span></li></ul><p><br></p><p><span style="font-family: Arial; font-size: 10.5pt;">We are constantly on the lookout for Community influencers who take pride in their contributions and truly believe in sharing knowledge and helping others. We will certainly notice you! Trailblazers is a formal influencer program that you will need to be invited to.</span></p><p><br></p><p><span style="font-family: Arial; font-size: 10.5pt;">Keep up the good work and connect with us if you want your contributions to be recognized and rewarded!</span></p><p><br></p><p><a href="https://community.zscaler.com/zenith/s/question/0D54u00009ezFpyCAE/step-8-a-few-more-things-to-consider" rel="noopener noreferrer" target="_blank">UP NEXT - A few more things to consider</a></p><p><br></p><p><br></p>
<!-- /ZS-POST -->

<!-- ZS-POST {"url":"https://community.zscaler.com/s/question/0D54u00009ezFwBCAU/step-6-accepted-solutions-select-as-best","lastmod":"2023-09-23T13:47:56.000Z","id":"0D54u00009ezFwBCAU"} -->
## STEP 6 - Accepted Solutions - “Select as Best”

- Source: https://community.zscaler.com/s/question/0D54u00009ezFwBCAU/step-6-accepted-solutions-select-as-best
- Type: Q&A
- Posted: 2023-06-03T18:10:58.000Z
- Last activity: 2023-09-23T13:47:56.000Z
- Answers: 0
- Likes: 1
- Note: ユーザー投稿であり Zscaler の公式見解ではない。内容が古い場合があるため投稿日を確認すること。
- Note: 回答本文は UI API では取得できないため未収録。

<p><strong style="font-family: Arial; font-size: 10.5pt;">“Select as Best”&nbsp; </strong><span style="font-family: Arial; font-size: 10.5pt;">is a fabulous way to help our community members with the answers they need. It also recognizes the active and diligent members who invested their time in responding to the post.</span></p><p><br></p><p><span style="font-family: Arial; font-size: 10.5pt;">Accepting a solution by “Selecting as Best” answer is very easy on our platform. Here is a step-by-step tutorial on how you can accept the right answers to your questions as solutions.</span></p><p><br></p><p><strong style="font-family: Arial; font-size: 10.5pt;"><em>NOTE: Only Authors of the posts and moderators/admins can accept responses as solutions.</em></strong></p><p><br></p><p><span style="font-family: Arial; font-size: 10.5pt;">Click on your post/topic</span></p><p><br></p><p><img src="/zenith/sfc/servlet.shepherd/version/download/0684u00000aBFLv?asPdf=false&amp;operationContext=CHATTER"></p><p><br></p><p><span style="font-family: Arial; font-size: 10.5pt;">Once the author of the post selects a response as the best, it becomes the accepted solution to the thread</span></p><p><br></p><p><img src="/zenith/sfc/servlet.shepherd/version/download/0684u00000aBFM0?asPdf=false&amp;operationContext=CHATTER"></p><p><br></p><p><span style="font-family: Arial; font-size: 10.5pt;">Please try to adopt the good habit of accepting solutions on your questions - this helps the users find answers and increases site credibility for all users.</span></p><p><br></p><p><a href="https://community.zscaler.com/zenith/s/question/0D54u00009ezFsTCAU/step-7-how-to-become-a-trailblazer-influencer" rel="noopener noreferrer" target="_blank">UP NEXT - How to become a Trailblazer Influencer</a></p><p><br></p><p><br></p>
<!-- /ZS-POST -->

<!-- ZS-POST {"url":"https://community.zscaler.com/s/question/0D54u00009ezG1FCAU/step-5-how-to-post-content","lastmod":"2026-08-14T13:36:19.000Z","id":"0D54u00009ezG1FCAU"} -->
## STEP 5 - How to post content

- Source: https://community.zscaler.com/s/question/0D54u00009ezG1FCAU/step-5-how-to-post-content
- Type: Q&A
- Posted: 2023-06-03T18:16:00.000Z
- Last activity: 2026-08-14T13:36:19.000Z
- Answers: 2
- Likes: 1
- Note: ユーザー投稿であり Zscaler の公式見解ではない。内容が古い場合があるため投稿日を確認すること。
- Note: 回答本文は UI API では取得できないため未収録。

<p><strong style="font-family: Arial; font-size: 10.5pt;"><em>Note: You have to be logged in to create content</em></strong></p><p><br></p><p><strong style="font-family: Arial; font-size: 10.5pt;">From the homepage:</strong></p><p><span style="font-family: Arial; font-size: 10.5pt;">Click on the “Start a Conversation” button to bring up the form to create a post</span></p><p><br></p><p><img src="/zenith/sfc/servlet.shepherd/version/download/0684u00000aBFM5?asPdf=false&amp;operationContext=CHATTER"></p><p><br></p><p><strong style="font-family: Arial; font-size: 10.5pt;">From a post:</strong></p><p><span style="font-family: Arial; font-size: 10.5pt;">Click on “Answer”&nbsp; to respond to the primary post content</span></p><p><br></p><p><img src="/zenith/sfc/servlet.shepherd/version/download/0684u00000aBFMA?asPdf=false&amp;operationContext=CHATTER"></p><p><br></p><p><strong style="font-family: Arial; font-size: 10.5pt;">From any area in the Community:</strong></p><p><span style="font-family: Arial; font-size: 10.5pt;">Look for the “Start a conversation” button to create a new post</span></p><p><br></p><p><img src="/zenith/sfc/servlet.shepherd/version/download/0684u00000aBFMF?asPdf=false&amp;operationContext=CHATTER"></p><p><br></p><p><br></p><p><span style="font-family: Arial; font-size: 10.5pt;">On the Create a Post dialog box, you can select the category and topic of where you want your post to appear, and you can use the rich text editing box to create a post before saving and publishing your post with the create button.&nbsp;</span></p><p><br></p><p><img src="/zenith/sfc/servlet.shepherd/version/download/0684u00000aBFMK?asPdf=false&amp;operationContext=CHATTER"></p><p><span style="font-family: Arial; font-size: 10.5pt;">Your content will post instantly on the Community. If anyone responds to your post, you will get a notification.</span></p><p><br></p><p><a href="https://community.zscaler.com/zenith/s/question/0D54u00009ezFwBCAU/step-6-accepted-solutions-select-as-best" rel="noopener noreferrer" target="_blank">UP NEXT - Accepted Solutions</a></p><p><br></p><p><br></p>
<!-- /ZS-POST -->
